========================================================================
FILE: src/world/fields/metric-tensor.js
========================================================================


class MetricTensor {

    constructor(values = null) {

        this.values = values
            ? Array.from(values)
            : [
                1, 0, 0,
                0, 1, 0,
                0, 0, 1
            ];

        this.validate();

    }

    validate() {

        if (this.values.length !== 9) {
            throw new Error("MetricTensor requires 9 values.");
        }

        if (!this.values.every(Number.isFinite)) {
            throw new Error("Metric contains non-finite values.");
        }

        const m = this.values;
        const tolerance = 1e-10;

        if (
            Math.abs(m[1] - m[3]) > tolerance ||
            Math.abs(m[2] - m[6]) > tolerance ||
            Math.abs(m[5] - m[7]) > tolerance
        ) {
            throw new Error("Metric must be symmetric.");
        }

        this.cholesky();

    }

    cholesky() {

        const m = this.values;

        const L = new Array(9).fill(0);

        for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {

            for (let j = 0; j <= i; j++) {

                let sum = m[i * 3 + j];

                for (let k = 0; k < j; k++) {

                    sum -=
                        L[i * 3 + k] *
                        L[j * 3 + k];

                }

                if (i === j) {

                    if (sum <= 0 || !Number.isFinite(sum)) {
                        throw new Error(
                            "Metric must be positive definite."
                        );
                    }

                    L[i * 3 + j] = Math.sqrt(sum);

                } else {

                    L[i * 3 + j] =
                        sum / L[j * 3 + j];

                }

            }

        }

        return L;

    }

    determinant() {

        const L = this.cholesky();

        const d =
            L[0] * L[4] * L[8];

        return d * d;

    }

    quadraticForm(vector) {

        const m = this.values;

        const x = [vector.x, vector.y, vector.z];

        let result = 0;

        for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {

            for (let j = 0; j < 3; j++) {

                result +=
                    x[i] * m[i * 3 + j] * x[j];

            }

        }

        return result;

    }

    norm(vector) {

        return Math.sqrt(
            Math.max(0, this.quadraticForm(vector))
        );

    }

    static identity() {

        return new MetricTensor();

    }

    static interpolate(a, b, t) {

        const A = a.cholesky();
        const B = b.cholesky();

        const L = new Array(9).fill(0);

        for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {

            L[i] =
                A[i] + (B[i] - A[i]) * t;

        }

        const values = new Array(9).fill(0);

        // G = L * transpose(L)

        for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {

            for (let j = 0; j < 3; j++) {

                for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {

                    values[i * 3 + j] +=
                        L[i * 3 + k] *
                        L[j * 3 + k];

                }

            }

        }

        return new MetricTensor(values);

    }

}

export { MetricTensor };

class PhysicalProperties {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.density = options.density ?? 1.0;

        this.hardness = options.hardness ?? 0.5;

        this.cohesion = options.cohesion ?? 0.5;

        this.thermalConductivity =
            options.thermalConductivity ?? 0.5;

        this.heatCapacity =
            options.heatCapacity ?? 1.0;

        this.elasticity =
            options.elasticity ?? 0.5;

        this.porosity =
            options.porosity ?? 0.3;

        this.moistureCapacity =
            options.moistureCapacity ?? 0.5;

        this.validate();

    }

    validate() {

        for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(this)) {

            if (
                typeof value === "number" &&
                (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0)
            ) {

                throw new Error(
                    `Invalid physical property: ${key}`
                );

            }

        }

    }

    clone() {

        return new PhysicalProperties(this);

    }

}

export { PhysicalProperties };


========================================================================
FILE: src/world/fields/spacetime-field.js
========================================================================


import { WorldField } from "./world-field.js";
import { MetricTensor } from "./metric-tensor.js";

class SpacetimeField extends WorldField {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        super("spacetime", options);

        this.metricProvider =
            options.metricProvider || null;

        this.lapseProvider =
            options.lapseProvider || null;

        this.defaultMetric =
            options.defaultMetric || MetricTensor.identity();

        this.defaultLapse =
            options.defaultLapse ?? 1.0;

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(this.defaultLapse) ||
            this.defaultLapse <= 0
        ) {
            throw new Error("Lapse must be positive.");
        }

    }

    evaluate(position, time = 0) {

        const metric = this.metricProvider
            ? this.metricProvider(position, time)
            : this.defaultMetric;

        const lapse = this.lapseProvider
            ? this.lapseProvider(position, time)
            : this.defaultLapse;

        if (!(metric instanceof MetricTensor)) {
            throw new TypeError("Invalid spatial metric.");
        }

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(lapse) ||
            lapse <= 0
        ) {
            throw new Error("Invalid temporal lapse.");
        }

        return {

            metric,

            lapse,

            coordinateTime: time,

            properTimeRate: lapse

        };

    }

    getProperTime(position, coordinateDelta, time = 0) {

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(coordinateDelta) ||
            coordinateDelta < 0
        ) {
            throw new RangeError("Invalid time interval.");
        }

        return this.sample(position, time).lapse *
            coordinateDelta;

    }

}

export { SpacetimeField };


========================================================================
FILE: src/world/fields/spatial-lattice.js
========================================================================


class SpatialLattice {

    constructor({
        origin = { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 },
        dimensions = { x: 16, y: 16, z: 16 },
        cellSize = 10,
        defaultValue = null,
        interpolate = null
    } = {}) {

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(cellSize) ||
            cellSize <= 0
        ) {
            throw new Error("Invalid lattice cell size.");
        }

        this.origin = { ...origin };

        this.dimensions = { ...dimensions };

        this.cellSize = cellSize;

        this.defaultValue = defaultValue;

        this.interpolate = interpolate ||
            SpatialLattice.interpolateValues;

        this.values = new Map();

    }

    getKey(x, y, z) {

        return `${x},${y},${z}`;

    }

    set(x, y, z, value) {

        this.validateCoordinates(x, y, z);

        this.values.set(
            this.getKey(x, y, z),
            value
        );

    }

    get(x, y, z) {

        return this.values.get(
            this.getKey(x, y, z)
        ) ?? this.defaultValue;

    }

    validateCoordinates(x, y, z) {

        const d = this.dimensions;

        if (
            !Number.isInteger(x) ||
            !Number.isInteger(y) ||
            !Number.isInteger(z) ||
            x < 0 || y < 0 || z < 0 ||
            x >= d.x || y >= d.y || z >= d.z
        ) {

            throw new RangeError(
                "Lattice coordinates out of bounds."
            );

        }

    }

    worldToGrid(position) {

        return {

            x: (position.x - this.origin.x) / this.cellSize,

            y: (position.y - this.origin.y) / this.cellSize,

            z: (position.z - this.origin.z) / this.cellSize

        };

    }

    sample(position) {

        const p = this.worldToGrid(position);

        const x0 = Math.floor(p.x);
        const y0 = Math.floor(p.y);
        const z0 = Math.floor(p.z);

        const tx = p.x - x0;
        const ty = p.y - y0;
        const tz = p.z - z0;

        const d = this.dimensions;

        if (
            p.x < 0 || p.y < 0 || p.z < 0 ||
            p.x > d.x - 1 ||
            p.y > d.y - 1 ||
            p.z > d.z - 1
        ) {
            return this.defaultValue;
        }

        const x1 = Math.min(x0 + 1, d.x - 1);
        const y1 = Math.min(y0 + 1, d.y - 1);
        const z1 = Math.min(z0 + 1, d.z - 1);

        const samples = [

            [this.get(x0, y0, z0), (1-tx)*(1-ty)*(1-tz)],
            [this.get(x1, y0, z0), tx*(1-ty)*(1-tz)],
            [this.get(x0, y1, z0), (1-tx)*ty*(1-tz)],
            [this.get(x1, y1, z0), tx*ty*(1-tz)],

            [this.get(x0, y0, z1), (1-tx)*(1-ty)*tz],
            [this.get(x1, y0, z1), tx*(1-ty)*tz],
            [this.get(x0, y1, z1), (1-tx)*ty*tz],
            [this.get(x1, y1, z1), tx*ty*tz]

        ];

        return this.interpolate(samples);

    }

    static interpolateValues(samples) {

	if (samples.some(([value]) => value == null)) {
		return null;
	}
        const first = samples[0][0];

        if (first === null || first === undefined) {
            return null;
        }

        if (typeof first === "number") {

            return samples.reduce(
                (sum, [value, weight]) =>
                    sum + value * weight,
                0
            );

        }

        if (Array.isArray(first)) {

            return first.map((_, i) =>

                samples.reduce(
                    (sum, [value, weight]) =>
                        sum + value[i] * weight,
                    0
                )

            );

        }

        if (
            typeof first === "object" &&
            "x" in first &&
            "y" in first &&
            "z" in first
        ) {

            const result = {
                x: 0,
                y: 0,
                z: 0
            };

            for (const [value, weight] of samples) {

                if (!value) continue;

                result.x += value.x * weight;
                result.y += value.y * weight;
                result.z += value.z * weight;

            }

            return result;

        }

        return first;

    }

}

export { SpatialLattice };

FILE: src/world/fields/temperature-field.js
========================================================================


import { WorldField } from "./world-field.js";

class TemperatureField extends WorldField {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        super("temperature", options);

        this.baseTemperature =
            options.baseTemperature ?? 15;

        this.referenceHeight =
            options.referenceHeight ?? 0;

        this.lapseRate =
            options.lapseRate ?? 0.0065;

        this.spatialVariation =
            options.spatialVariation || null;

        this.temporalVariation =
            options.temporalVariation || null;

    }

    evaluate(position, time = 0) {

        const altitudeDifference =
            position.y - this.referenceHeight;

        let temperature =
            this.baseTemperature -
            altitudeDifference * this.lapseRate;

        if (this.spatialVariation) {

            temperature +=
                this.spatialVariation(position, time);

        }

        if (this.temporalVariation) {

            temperature +=
                this.temporalVariation(position, time);

        }

        return {

            value: temperature,

            unit: "celsius",

            position,

            time

        };

    }

}

export { TemperatureField };


========================================================================
FILE: src/world/fields/world-field-system.js
========================================================================


import { FieldRegistry } from "./field-registry.js";
import { FieldSampler } from "./field-sampler.js";

import { SpacetimeField } from "./spacetime-field.js";
import { TemperatureField } from "./temperature-field.js";
import { MaterialField } from "./material-field.js";

import { MetricTensor } from "./metric-tensor.js";

import { EuclideanGeometry } from "./geometry/euclidean-geometry.js";
import { PoincareGeometry } from "./geometry/poincare-geometry.js";
import { GeodesicSolver } from "./geometry/geodesic-solver.js";


class WorldFieldSystem {

    constructor(options = {}) {

        this.terrain = options.terrain || null;

        this.geologicalField =
            options.geologicalField || null;

        this.registry = new FieldRegistry();

        this.sampler = new FieldSampler(
            this.registry
        );

        this.geometries = new Map();

        // Default geometry.
        this.geometries.set(
            "euclidean",
            new EuclideanGeometry()
        );

        this.geometries.set(
            "poincare",
            new PoincareGeometry(-1)
        );

        this.activeGeometry =
            this.geometries.get("euclidean");

        // Initial spacetime configuration.
        this.spacetime = new SpacetimeField({

            defaultMetric: MetricTensor.identity(),

            defaultLapse: 1.0

        });

        // Initial environmental fields.
        this.temperature = new TemperatureField(
            options.temperature || {}
        );

        this.material = this.geologicalField
            ? new MaterialField(
                this.geologicalField,
                options.material || {}
            )
            : null;

        // Register fields.
        this.registry.register(this.spacetime);

        this.registry.register(this.temperature);

        if (this.material) {

            this.registry.register(this.material);

        }

    }

    registerField(field) {

        return this.registry.register(field);

    }

    unregisterField(name) {

        return this.registry.unregister(name);

    }

    getField(name) {

        return this.registry.get(name);

    }

    sample(name, position, time = 0) {

        return this.sampler.sample(
            name,
            position,
            time
        );

    }

    sampleAll(position, time = 0) {

        return this.sampler.sampleAll(
            position,
            time
        );

    }

    sampleEnvironment(position, time = 0) {

        return {

            spacetime: this.sample(
                "spacetime",
                position,
                time
            ),

            temperature: this.sample(
                "temperature",
                position,
                time
            ),

            material: this.sample(
                "material",
                position,
                time
            )

        };

    }

    setGeometry(name) {

        const geometry = this.geometries.get(name);

        if (!geometry) {

            throw new Error(
                `Unknown geometry model: ${name}`
            );

        }

        this.activeGeometry = geometry;

        return geometry;

    }

    registerGeometry(name, geometry) {

        if (!geometry || typeof geometry.metricAt !== "function") {

            throw new TypeError("Invalid geometry model.");

        }

        this.geometries.set(name, geometry);

        return geometry;

    }

    getGeometry(name = null) {

        if (!name) return this.activeGeometry;

        return this.geometries.get(name) || null;

    }

    createGeodesicSolver(options = {}) {

        return new GeodesicSolver(
            this.activeGeometry,
            options
        );

    }

    getSpatialMetric(position) {

        return this.activeGeometry.metricAt(position);

    }

    getProperTime(position, coordinateDelta, time = 0) {

        return this.spacetime.getProperTime(
            position,
            coordinateDelta,
            time
        );

    }

    dispose() {

        this.registry.clear();

        this.geometries.clear();

    }

}

export { WorldFieldSystem };

FILE: src/world/fields/world-field.js

class WorldField {

    constructor(name, options = {}) {

        if (!name) {
            throw new Error("WorldField requires a name.");
        }

        this.name = name;

        this.enabled = options.enabled ?? true;

        this.priority = options.priority ?? 0;

        this.metadata = options.metadata || {};

    }

    sample(position, time = 0) {

        if (!this.enabled) return null;

        if (!position) return null;

        return this.evaluate(position, time);

    }

    evaluate() {

        throw new Error(
            "WorldField.evaluate() must be implemented."
        );

    }

}

export { WorldField };

        `;

	this.launcher = document.createElement("button");

this.launcher.textContent = "WORLD LIBRARY";

Object.assign(this.launcher.style, {
    position: "fixed",
    top: "76px",
    right: "18px",
    zIndex: "1500",
    padding: "12px 18px",

document.body.appendChild(this.launcher);

        this.list = this.container.querySelector("#world-list");
        this.nameInput = this.container.querySelector("#world-name");
        this.status = this.container.querySelector("#world-status");

    }

    open() {
        this.container.style.display = "flex";
        this.refresh();
    }

    close() {
        this.container.style.display = "none";
    }

    async refresh() {
        try {
            const worlds = await this.persistence.listWorlds();

            this.list.replaceChildren();

            if (worlds.length === 0) {
                const empty = document.createElement("p");
                empty.textContent = "No saved worlds yet.";
                this.list.appendChild(empty);
                return;
            }

            for (const world of worlds) {
                const row = document.createElement("div");

                Object.assign(row.style, {
                    display: "flex",
                    alignItems: "center",
                    gap: "8px",
                    padding: "12px 0",
                    borderBottom: "1px solid #354252"
                });

                const information = document.createElement("div");
                information.style.flex = "1";

                const title = document.createElement("strong");
                title.textContent = world.name;

                const details = document.createElement("small");
                details.style.display = "block";
                details.style.color = "#aab5c4";
                details.textContent =
                    `Seed: ${world.worldSeed} · Revision: ${world.revision}`;

                information.append(title, details);

                const load = document.createElement("button");
                load.textContent = "LOAD";
                load.onclick = () => this.loadWorld(world.id);

                const rename = document.createElement("button");
                rename.textContent = "RENAME";
                rename.onclick = () => this.renameWorld(world);

                const remove = document.createElement("button");
                remove.textContent = "DELETE";
                remove.onclick = () => this.deleteWorld(world);

                row.append(information, load, rename, remove);
                this.list.appendChild(row);
            }
        } catch (error) {
            this.setStatus(error.message);
        }
    }

    async createWorld() {
        try {
            const name = this.nameInput.value.trim();

            const world = await this.persistence.createWorld(
                name,
                {
                    worldSeed: Math.floor(
                        Math.random() * 2147483647
                    )
                }
            );

            this.currentWorldId = world.metadata.id;

            if (this.callbacks.onLoad) {
                await this.callbacks.onLoad(world);
            }

            this.nameInput.value = "";
            this.setStatus(`Created ${name}.`);
            await this.refresh();
        } catch (error) {
            this.setStatus(error.message);
        }
    }

    async loadWorld(id) {
        try {
            const world = await this.persistence.loadWorld(id);

            if (this.callbacks.onLoad) {
                await this.callbacks.onLoad(world);
            }

            this.currentWorldId = id;
            this.setStatus(`Loaded ${world.metadata.name}.`);
            await this.refresh();
        } catch (error) {
            this.setStatus(error.message);
        }
    }

    async saveCurrent() {
        try {
            if (!this.currentWorldId) {
                throw new Error("Select or create a world first.");
            }

            const world = await this.persistence.loadWorld(
                this.currentWorldId
            );

            if (this.callbacks.onCapture) {
                await this.callbacks.onCapture(world);
            }

            await this.persistence.saveWorld(world);

            this.setStatus(
                `Saved ${world.metadata.name} successfully.`
            );

            await this.refresh();
        } catch (error) {
            this.setStatus(error.message);
        }
    }

    async renameWorld(world) {
        const name = prompt(
            "Enter a new world name:",
            world.name
        );

        if (name === null) return;

        try {
            await this.persistence.renameWorld(
                world.id,
                name
            );

            await this.refresh();
        } catch (error) {
            this.setStatus(error.message);
        }
    }

    async deleteWorld(world) {
        if (!confirm(`Delete "${world.name}" permanently?`)) {
            return;
        }

        try {
            await this.persistence.deleteWorld(world.id);

            if (this.currentWorldId === world.id) {
                this.currentWorldId = null;
            }

            this.setStatus(`Deleted ${world.name}.`);
            await this.refresh();
        } catch (error) {
            this.setStatus(error.message);
        }
    }

dispose() {
    this.launcher?.remove();
    this.container?.remove();

    this.launcher = null;
    this.container = null;
}

}

export { WorldLibraryUI };


========================================================================
FILE: src/world/persistence/world-persistence.js
========================================================================


import { WorldState } from "./world-state.js";

const DATABASE_NAME = "somnium-worlds";
const DATABASE_VERSION = 1;
const STORE_NAME = "worlds";

class WorldPersistence {
    constructor() {
        this.database = null;
        this.opening = null;
    }

    async open() {
        if (this.database) {
            return this.database;
        }

        if (this.opening) {
            return this.opening;
        }

        if (!globalThis.indexedDB) {
            throw new Error(
                "IndexedDB is unavailable in this browser."
            );
        }

        this.opening = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            const request = indexedDB.open(
                DATABASE_NAME,
                DATABASE_VERSION
            );

            request.onupgradeneeded = () => {
                const database = request.result;

                if (!database.objectStoreNames.contains(STORE_NAME)) {
                    database.createObjectStore(STORE_NAME, {
                        keyPath: "metadata.id"
                    });
                }
            };

            request.onsuccess = () => {
                this.database = request.result;

                this.database.onversionchange = () => {
                    this.database.close();
                    this.database = null;
                };

                resolve(this.database);
            };

            request.onerror = () => {
                reject(request.error);
            };

            request.onblocked = () => {
                console.warn(
                    "[WorldPersistence] Database upgrade blocked."
                );
            };
        });

        try {
            return await this.opening;
        } finally {
            this.opening = null;
        }
    }

    async transaction(mode, operation) {
        const database = await this.open();

        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            const tx = database.transaction(STORE_NAME, mode);
            const store = tx.objectStore(STORE_NAME);

            let result;

            try {
                result = operation(store, tx);
            } catch (error) {
                tx.abort();
                reject(error);
                return;
            }

            tx.oncomplete = () => resolve(result);
            tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
            tx.onabort = () => reject(
                tx.error ?? new Error("Transaction aborted.")
            );
        });
    }

    async createWorld(name, options = {}) {
        const cleanName = String(name ?? "").trim();

        if (!cleanName) {
            throw new Error("Please provide a world name.");
        }

        const world = new WorldState({
            ...options,
            name: cleanName
        });

        world.validate();

        await this.transaction("readwrite", store => {
            store.add(world.serialize());
        });

        console.log(
            "[WorldPersistence] Created world:",
            world.metadata.name
        );

        return world;
    }

    async listWorlds() {
        const records = await new Promise(async (resolve, reject) => {
            try {
                const database = await this.open();
                const tx = database.transaction(STORE_NAME, "readonly");
                const request = tx.objectStore(STORE_NAME).getAll();

                request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
                request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
            } catch (error) {
                reject(error);
            }
        });

        return records
            .map(record => ({
                id: record.metadata.id,
                name: record.metadata.name,
                createdAt: record.metadata.createdAt,
                updatedAt: record.metadata.updatedAt,
                revision: record.metadata.revision,
                worldSeed: record.generation.worldSeed
            }))
            .sort((a, b) =>
                b.updatedAt.localeCompare(a.updatedAt)
            );
    }

    async loadWorld(id) {
        const record = await new Promise(async (resolve, reject) => {
            try {
                const database = await this.open();
                const tx = database.transaction(STORE_NAME, "readonly");
                const request = tx.objectStore(STORE_NAME).get(id);

                request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
                request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
            } catch (error) {
                reject(error);
            }
        });

        if (!record) {
            throw new Error(`World not found: ${id}`);
        }

        return WorldState.deserialize(record);
    }

    async saveWorld(world, options = {}) {
        if (!(world instanceof WorldState)) {
            throw new TypeError("Expected a WorldState instance.");
        }

        world.validate();

        const serialized = world.serialize();

        await this.transaction("readwrite", (store, tx) => {
            const request = store.get(world.metadata.id);

            request.onsuccess = () => {
                const existing = request.result;

                if (!existing) {
                    if (!options.allowCreate) {
                        tx.abort();
                        return;
                    }

                    world.touch();
                    store.add(world.serialize());
                    return;
                }

                // Prevent overwriting changes from another tab.
                if (
                    existing.metadata.revision !==
                    world.metadata.revision
                ) {
                    tx.abort();
                    return;
                }

                world.touch();
                store.put(world.serialize());
            };
        });

        return world;
    }

    async renameWorld(id, newName) {
        const world = await this.loadWorld(id);
        const cleanName = String(newName ?? "").trim();

        if (!cleanName) {
            throw new Error("World name cannot be empty.");
        }

        world.metadata.name = cleanName;

        return this.saveWorld(world);
    }

    async deleteWorld(id) {
        await this.transaction("readwrite", store => {
            store.delete(id);
        });

        console.log("[WorldPersistence] Deleted world:", id);
    }

    close() {
        this.database?.close();
        this.database = null;
    }
}

export { WorldPersistence };

FILE: src/world/persistence/world-session.js

import { WorldState } from "./world-state.js";

class WorldSession {
    constructor(ecology) {
        if (!ecology?.populationManager) {
            throw new Error(
                "WorldSession requires ChunkEcologySystem."
            );
        }

        this.ecology = ecology;
    }

    capture(world, elapsedTime = 0) {
        if (!(world instanceof WorldState)) {
            throw new TypeError("Expected WorldState.");
        }

        const manager = this.ecology.populationManager;

        const chunkRecords = [];

        for (const record of manager.records.values()) {
            const exported = manager.exportRecord(
                record.x,
                record.z
            );

            if (exported) {
                chunkRecords.push(exported);
            }
        }

        world.generation.worldSeed = manager.worldSeed;

        world.simulation.elapsedTime = elapsedTime;

        world.ecology.chunkRecords = chunkRecords;


        return world;
    }

    restore(world) {
        if (!(world instanceof WorldState)) {
            throw new TypeError("Expected WorldState.");
        }

        const ecology = this.ecology;
        const manager = ecology.populationManager;

        // Deactivate currently rendered populations.
        for (const record of manager.records.values()) {
            if (record.active) {
                manager.deactivateRecord(record);
            }
        }

        manager.records.clear();
        manager.pending.length = 0;
        manager.activeKeys.clear();
        manager.simulationCursor = 0;

        ecology.worldSeed = world.generation.worldSeed;
        manager.worldSeed = world.generation.worldSeed;

        // Restore all previously generated populations.
        for (const record of world.ecology.chunkRecords) {
            manager.importRecord(record);
        }

        ecology.renderDirty = true;

        return {
            elapsedTime: world.simulation.elapsedTime,
            worldSeed: world.generation.worldSeed,
            restoredChunks: world.ecology.chunkRecords.length
        };
    }

    getStats() {
        return {
            worldSeed: this.ecology.populationManager.worldSeed,
            generatedChunks:
                this.ecology.populationManager.records.size
        };
    }
}

export { WorldSession };

const WORLD_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1;

function createId() {
    if (globalThis.crypto?.randomUUID) {
        return globalThis.crypto.randomUUID();
    }

    return `world-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()
        .toString(36).slice(2)}`;
}

function cloneData(value) {
    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
}

class WorldState {
    constructor(options = {}) {
        const now = new Date().toISOString();

        this.schemaVersion = WORLD_SCHEMA_VERSION;

        this.metadata = {
            id: options.id ?? createId(),
            name: options.name ?? "Untitled World",
            createdAt: options.createdAt ?? now,
            updatedAt: now,
            revision: options.revision ?? 0
        };

        this.generation = {
            worldSeed: options.worldSeed ?? 91827,
            generatorVersion: options.generatorVersion ?? 1,
            configuration: options.configuration ?? {}
        };

        this.simulation = {
            elapsedTime: options.elapsedTime ?? 0,
            simulationRate: options.simulationRate ?? 1,
            paused: options.paused ?? false
        };

        this.ecology = {
            chunkRecords: options.chunkRecords ?? []
        };

        this.entities = {
            manual: options.manualEntities ?? [],
            overrides: options.entityOverrides ?? [],
            removedProceduralIds: options.removedProceduralIds ?? []
        };

        this.environment = {
            state: options.environmentState ?? {}
        };
    }

    validate() {
        if (!this.metadata.id || !this.metadata.name.trim()) {
            throw new Error("World requires an ID and name.");
        }

        if (!Number.isInteger(this.generation.worldSeed)) {
            throw new Error("Invalid world seed.");
        }

        if (
            !Number.isFinite(this.simulation.elapsedTime) ||
            this.simulation.elapsedTime < 0
        ) {
            throw new Error("Invalid simulation time.");
        }

        if (!Array.isArray(this.ecology.chunkRecords)) {
            throw new Error("Invalid ecology records.");
        }

        if (
            !Array.isArray(this.entities.manual) ||
            !Array.isArray(this.entities.overrides) ||
            !Array.isArray(this.entities.removedProceduralIds)
        ) {
            throw new Error("Invalid entity collections.");
        }

        return true;
    }

    touch() {
        this.metadata.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
        this.metadata.revision++;
    }

    serialize() {
        this.validate();

        return cloneData(this);
    }

    static deserialize(data) {
        if (!data || typeof data !== "object") {
            throw new Error("Invalid world data.");
        }

        if (data.schemaVersion !== WORLD_SCHEMA_VERSION) {
            throw new Error(
                `Unsupported world schema: ${data.schemaVersion}`
            );
        }

        const state = new WorldState({
            id: data.metadata?.id,
            name: data.metadata?.name,
            createdAt: data.metadata?.createdAt,
            revision: data.metadata?.revision,
            worldSeed: data.generation?.worldSeed,
            generatorVersion: data.generation?.generatorVersion,
            configuration: data.generation?.configuration,
            elapsedTime: data.simulation?.elapsedTime,
            simulationRate: data.simulation?.simulationRate,
            paused: data.simulation?.paused,
            chunkRecords: data.ecology?.chunkRecords,
            manualEntities: data.entities?.manual,
            entityOverrides: data.entities?.overrides,
            removedProceduralIds:
                data.entities?.removedProceduralIds,
            environmentState: data.environment?.state
        });

        state.metadata.updatedAt =
            data.metadata.updatedAt ?? state.metadata.updatedAt;

        state.validate();

        return state;
    }
}

export {
    WorldState,
    WORLD_SCHEMA_VERSION
};

FILE: src/world/slice-world-registry.js
