/** Owns runtime resources for independently addressed world slices. */

class SliceWorldRegistry {
    constructor() {
        this.slices = new Map();
        this.disposed = false;
    }

    register(slice, runtime = {}) {
        this.assertActive();
        if (!slice || typeof slice.id !== 'string' || !slice.id.trim()) {
            throw new TypeError('SliceWorldRegistry requires a valid WorldSlice.');
        }
        if (this.slices.has(slice.id)) throw new Error(`Slice runtime already registered: ${slice.id}`);
        const binding = { slice, ...runtime, disposed: false };
        this.slices.set(slice.id, binding);
        return binding;
    }

    get(sliceId) {
        this.assertActive();
        return this.slices.get(sliceId) ?? null;
    }

    require(sliceId) {
        const binding = this.get(sliceId);
        if (!binding) throw new Error(`Unknown slice runtime: ${sliceId}`);
        return binding;
    }

    getTransform(sliceId) {
        return this.require(sliceId).spatialTransform;
    }

    unregister(sliceId) {
        const binding = this.slices.get(sliceId);
        if (!binding) return false;
        disposeBinding(binding);
        this.slices.delete(sliceId);
        return true;
    }

    assertActive() {
        if (this.disposed) throw new Error('SliceWorldRegistry has been disposed.');
    }

    dispose() {
        if (this.disposed) return;
        for (const binding of this.slices.values()) disposeBinding(binding);
        this.slices.clear();
        this.disposed = true;
    }
}

function disposeBinding(binding) {
    if (binding.disposed) return;
    binding.disposed = true;
    if (typeof binding.dispose === 'function') binding.dispose();
    else if (binding.terrain?.dispose) binding.terrain.dispose();
}

export { SliceWorldRegistry };


========================================================================
FILE: src/world/spatial-chunk-graph.js
========================================================================

/** One chunk graph for ordinary 3D chunks and explicit 4D corridor chunks. */

import { createSliceAddress, getSliceAddressKey } from './fields/geometry/world-slice.js';
import { CorridorChunk } from './corridor-chunk.js';
import { ambientToSlice, dot4, subtract4 } from './fields/geometry/spatial-4d.js?v=somnium-20261109-phase17i-bidirectional-spherical-passage-1';
import { evaluateAnomalyInfluence, evaluateChunkReachability } from './fields/geometry/anomaly-influence-field.js?v=somnium-20261109-phase17i-bidirectional-spherical-passage-1';
import { evaluateOpticalAnomalyCoupling } from './fields/geometry/optical-contracts.js?v=somnium-20261109-phase17i-bidirectional-spherical-passage-1';

class SpatialChunkGraph {
    constructor(topology) {
        if (!topology || typeof topology.getCorridor !== 'function') {
            throw new TypeError('SpatialChunkGraph requires a WorldTopology.');
        }
        this.topology = topology;
        this.slices = new Map();
        this.loadedChunks = new Map();
        this.corridors = new Map();
        this.edges = new Map();
        this.disposed = false;
    }

    registerSlice(slice, options = {}) {
        this.assertActive();
        const record = typeof slice === 'string' ? this.topology.getSlice(slice) : slice;
        if (!record || typeof record.id !== 'string') throw new TypeError('A registered world slice is required.');
        this.topology.getSlice(record.id);
        const chunkSize = options.chunkSize ?? 32;
        if (!Number.isFinite(chunkSize) || chunkSize <= 0) throw new RangeError('Chunk size must be positive.');
        const current = this.slices.get(record.id);
        if (current && current.chunkSize !== chunkSize) throw new Error(`Chunk size already registered for ${record.id}.`);
        this.slices.set(record.id, { sliceId: record.id, chunkSize });
        return { ...this.slices.get(record.id) };
    }

    hasSlice(sliceId) {
        return this.slices.has(sliceId);
    }

    getAddress(sliceId, position) {
        const slice = this.requireSlice(sliceId);
        if (!position || !Number.isFinite(position.x) || !Number.isFinite(position.z)) {
            throw new TypeError('Chunk lookup requires a finite x/z position.');
        }
        return createSliceAddress(
            sliceId,
            Math.floor(position.x / slice.chunkSize),
            Math.floor(position.z / slice.chunkSize)
        );
    }

    registerChunk(address, chunk = null) {
        this.assertActive();
        const key = getSliceAddressKey(address);
        const normalized = createSliceAddress(address.sliceId, address.chunkX, address.chunkZ);
        this.requireSlice(normalized.sliceId);
        if (chunk) this.loadedChunks.set(key, chunk);
        this.refreshLoadedChunkNeighbors();
        return this.describeWorldChunk(normalized, key);
    }

    getChunk(address) {
        this.assertActive();
        const normalized = createSliceAddress(address.sliceId, address.chunkX, address.chunkZ);
        this.requireSlice(normalized.sliceId);
        return this.describeWorldChunk(normalized, getSliceAddressKey(normalized));
    }

    setChunkLoaded(address, chunk) {
        this.assertActive();
        const key = getSliceAddressKey(address);
        this.requireSlice(address.sliceId);
        if (chunk) this.loadedChunks.set(key, chunk);
        else this.loadedChunks.delete(key);
        this.refreshLoadedChunkNeighbors();
    }

    registerCorridor(corridorId) {
        this.assertActive();
        const corridor = typeof corridorId === 'string'
            ? this.topology.getCorridor(corridorId)
            : corridorId;
        if (!corridor || typeof corridor.id !== 'string') throw new TypeError('A valid corridor is required.');
        if (this.corridors.has(corridor.id)) throw new Error(`Corridor chunk already exists: ${corridor.id}`);

        const sourceAddress = this.getAddress(corridor.source.sliceId, corridor.source.position);
        const destinationAddress = this.getAddress(corridor.destination.sliceId, corridor.destination.position);
        const chunk = new CorridorChunk(corridor);
        const nodeKey = this.getCorridorKey(corridor.id);
        const sourceKey = getSliceAddressKey(sourceAddress);
        const destinationKey = getSliceAddressKey(destinationAddress);
        const link = {
            id: corridor.id,
            nodeKey,
            chunk,
            sourceAddress,
            destinationAddress,
            sourceKey,
            destinationKey
        };
        this.corridors.set(corridor.id, link);

        if (corridor.traversable) {
            this.addEdge(sourceKey, nodeKey, corridor.id, 1);
            this.addEdge(nodeKey, destinationKey, corridor.id, 1);
            if (corridor.bidirectional) {
                this.addEdge(destinationKey, nodeKey, corridor.id, -1);
                this.addEdge(nodeKey, sourceKey, corridor.id, -1);
            }
        }
        this.refreshLoadedChunkNeighbors();
        return chunk;
    }

    removeCorridor(corridorId) {
        const link = this.corridors.get(corridorId);
        if (!link) return false;
        this.removeEdges(link.nodeKey, corridorId);
        this.removeEdges(link.sourceKey, corridorId);
        this.removeEdges(link.destinationKey, corridorId);
        link.chunk.dispose();
        this.corridors.delete(corridorId);
        this.refreshLoadedChunkNeighbors();
        return true;
    }

    getCorridorChunk(corridorId) {
        return this.corridors.get(corridorId)?.chunk ?? null;
    }

    getNeighbors(address) {
        this.assertActive();
        if (typeof address === 'string' && address.startsWith('corridor:')) {
            return (this.edges.get(address) ?? []).map(function describeEdge(edge) {
                return this.describeNode(edge.to, edge);
            }, this);
        }
        const normalized = typeof address === 'string'
            ? parseAddressKey(address)
            : createSliceAddress(address.sliceId, address.chunkX, address.chunkZ);
        this.requireSlice(normalized.sliceId);
        const result = [
            { chunkX: normalized.chunkX, chunkZ: normalized.chunkZ - 1 },
            { chunkX: normalized.chunkX, chunkZ: normalized.chunkZ + 1 },
            { chunkX: normalized.chunkX + 1, chunkZ: normalized.chunkZ },
            { chunkX: normalized.chunkX - 1, chunkZ: normalized.chunkZ }
        ].map(function toNeighbor(coordinates) {
            const neighborAddress = createSliceAddress(normalized.sliceId, coordinates.chunkX, coordinates.chunkZ);
            return this.describeWorldChunk(neighborAddress, getSliceAddressKey(neighborAddress));
        }, this);
        for (const edge of this.edges.get(getSliceAddressKey(normalized)) ?? []) {
            result.push(this.describeNode(edge.to, edge));
        }
        return result;
    }

    /**
     * Evaluates same-slice and cross-slice chunk reachability through one
     * parameterized spatial/W-distance predicate. Different slices still need
     * an explicit traversable topology edge.
     */
    getReachability(sourceAddress, targetAddress, options = {}) {
        this.assertActive();
        const source = typeof sourceAddress === 'string' ? parseAddressKey(sourceAddress) :
            createSliceAddress(sourceAddress.sliceId, sourceAddress.chunkX, sourceAddress.chunkZ);
        const target = typeof targetAddress === 'string' ? parseAddressKey(targetAddress) :
            createSliceAddress(targetAddress.sliceId, targetAddress.chunkX, targetAddress.chunkZ);
        const maxChunkDistance = options.maxChunkDistance ?? 1;
        if (!Number.isFinite(maxChunkDistance) || maxChunkDistance < 0) {
            throw new RangeError('Maximum chunk distance must be non-negative and finite.');
        }

        if (source.sliceId === target.sliceId) {
            const spatialDistance = Math.abs(target.chunkX - source.chunkX) +
                Math.abs(target.chunkZ - source.chunkZ);
            const result = evaluateChunkReachability({
                spatialDistance, spatialReach: maxChunkDistance, wDistance: 0, wReach: 0
            });
            return { ...result, kind: 'same-slice', sourceAddress: source, targetAddress: target,
                anomalyId: null, freedom: 1 };
        }

        if (!options.position4D) {
            return unreachableResult('missing-ambient-position', source, target);
        }
        const position4D = options.position4D;
        const connection = this.topology.getTraversableNeighbors(source.sliceId).find((neighbor) =>
            neighbor.sliceId === target.sliceId &&
            (!options.corridorId || neighbor.corridorId === options.corridorId)
        );
        if (!connection) return unreachableResult('no-explicit-topology-edge', source, target);

        const anomaly = this.topology.getCorridor(connection.corridorId);
        if (anomaly.type !== 'slice-transition') {
            return unreachableResult('unsupported-cross-slice-reachability', source, target, anomaly.id);
        }
        const sourceSlice = this.topology.getSlice(source.sliceId);
        const targetSlice = this.topology.getSlice(target.sliceId);
        const projected = ambientToSlice(targetSlice.frame, position4D).position;
        const projectedAddress = this.getAddress(target.sliceId, projected);
        const spatialDistance = Math.abs(target.chunkX - projectedAddress.chunkX) +
            Math.abs(target.chunkZ - projectedAddress.chunkZ);
        const wDistance = Math.abs(dot4(subtract4(targetSlice.frame.origin, position4D), sourceSlice.frame.normal));
        const influence = evaluateAnomalyInfluence(anomaly, position4D, options);
        const result = evaluateChunkReachability({
            spatialDistance, spatialReach: maxChunkDistance, wDistance, wReach: influence.wReach
        });
        return {
            ...result,
            kind: 'cross-slice',
            sourceAddress: source,
            targetAddress: target,
            projectedAddress,
            projectedPosition: projected,
            anomalyId: anomaly.id,
            freedom: influence.freedom,
            influenceDistance: influence.distance,
            maxWReach: anomaly.influence.maxWReach
        };
    }

    /**
     * Optical cross-slice reachability uses explicit optical permissions and
     * the independent optical W-coupling field. It never consults traversable
     * flags or player influence modifiers.
     */
    getOpticalReachability(sourceAddress, targetAddress, options = {}) {
        this.assertActive();
        const source = typeof sourceAddress === 'string' ? parseAddressKey(sourceAddress) :
            createSliceAddress(sourceAddress.sliceId, sourceAddress.chunkX, sourceAddress.chunkZ);
        const target = typeof targetAddress === 'string' ? parseAddressKey(targetAddress) :
            createSliceAddress(targetAddress.sliceId, targetAddress.chunkX, targetAddress.chunkZ);
        const maxChunkDistance = options.maxChunkDistance ?? 1;
        if (!Number.isFinite(maxChunkDistance) || maxChunkDistance < 0) {
            throw new RangeError('Maximum optical chunk distance must be non-negative and finite.');
        }
        if (source.sliceId === target.sliceId) {
            const spatialDistance = Math.abs(target.chunkX - source.chunkX) +
                Math.abs(target.chunkZ - source.chunkZ);
            return {
                ...evaluateChunkReachability({
                    spatialDistance, spatialReach: maxChunkDistance, wDistance: 0, wReach: 0
                }),
                kind: 'same-slice', sourceAddress: source, targetAddress: target,
                anomalyId: null, opticalCoupling: 1
            };
        }
        if (!options.position4D) return unreachableResult('missing-ambient-position', source, target);
        const connection = this.topology.getOpticallyConnectedNeighbors(source.sliceId).find((neighbor) =>
            neighbor.sliceId === target.sliceId &&
            (!options.corridorId || neighbor.corridorId === options.corridorId)
        );
        if (!connection) return unreachableResult('no-optical-topology-edge', source, target);

        const anomaly = this.topology.getCorridor(connection.corridorId);
        const sourceSlice = this.topology.getSlice(source.sliceId);
        const targetSlice = this.topology.getSlice(target.sliceId);
        const projected = ambientToSlice(targetSlice.frame, options.position4D).position;
        const projectedAddress = this.getAddress(target.sliceId, projected);
        const spatialDistance = Math.abs(target.chunkX - projectedAddress.chunkX) +
            Math.abs(target.chunkZ - projectedAddress.chunkZ);
        const wDistance = Math.abs(dot4(
            subtract4(targetSlice.frame.origin, options.position4D), sourceSlice.frame.normal
        ));
        const influence = evaluateOpticalAnomalyCoupling(
            anomaly, options.couplingPosition4D ?? options.position4D
        );
        const result = evaluateChunkReachability({
            spatialDistance, spatialReach: maxChunkDistance,
            wDistance, wReach: influence.opticalWReach
        });
        return {
            ...result,
            kind: 'optical-cross-slice',
            sourceAddress: source,
            targetAddress: target,
            projectedAddress,
            projectedPosition: projected,
            anomalyId: anomaly.id,
            opticalCoupling: influence.coupling,
            opticalWReach: influence.opticalWReach,
            maxWOpticalReach: anomaly.optics.maxWOpticalReach
        };
    }

    /** Returns explicitly connected target-slice chunk candidates reachable by light. */
    getOpticallyReachableChunkCandidates(sourceSliceId, position4D, options = {}) {
        this.assertActive();
        const sourceSlice = this.topology.getSlice(sourceSliceId);
        const sourceLocal = ambientToSlice(sourceSlice.frame, position4D).position;
        const sourceAddress = this.getAddress(sourceSliceId, sourceLocal);
        const maxChunkDistance = options.maxChunkDistance ?? 1;
        const candidates = new Map();
        for (const neighbor of this.topology.getOpticallyConnectedNeighbors(sourceSliceId)) {
            if (options.targetSliceId && neighbor.sliceId !== options.targetSliceId) continue;
            const targetSlice = this.topology.getSlice(neighbor.sliceId);
            const projected = ambientToSlice(targetSlice.frame, position4D).position;
            const projectedAddress = this.getAddress(neighbor.sliceId, projected);
            for (let offsetX = -Math.ceil(maxChunkDistance); offsetX <= Math.ceil(maxChunkDistance); offsetX++) {
                for (let offsetZ = -Math.ceil(maxChunkDistance); offsetZ <= Math.ceil(maxChunkDistance); offsetZ++) {
                    const address = createSliceAddress(
                        neighbor.sliceId, projectedAddress.chunkX + offsetX,
                        projectedAddress.chunkZ + offsetZ
                    );
                    const reachability = this.getOpticalReachability(sourceAddress, address, {
                        ...options, position4D, maxChunkDistance, corridorId: neighbor.corridorId
                    });
                    if (reachability.reachable) {
                        candidates.set(getSliceAddressKey(address), { address, reachability });
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return Array.from(candidates.values());
    }

    /** Returns the target-slice chunks currently reachable from an ambient point. */
    getReachableChunkCandidates(sourceSliceId, position4D, options = {}) {
        this.assertActive();
        const sourceSlice = this.topology.getSlice(sourceSliceId);
        const sourceLocal = ambientToSlice(sourceSlice.frame, position4D).position;
        const sourceAddress = this.getAddress(sourceSliceId, sourceLocal);
        const maxChunkDistance = options.maxChunkDistance ?? 1;
        const candidates = new Map();

        for (const neighbor of this.topology.getTraversableNeighbors(sourceSliceId)) {
            if (options.targetSliceId && neighbor.sliceId !== options.targetSliceId) continue;
            const targetSlice = this.topology.getSlice(neighbor.sliceId);
            const projected = ambientToSlice(targetSlice.frame, position4D).position;
            const projectedAddress = this.getAddress(neighbor.sliceId, projected);
            for (let offsetX = -Math.ceil(maxChunkDistance); offsetX <= Math.ceil(maxChunkDistance); offsetX++) {
                for (let offsetZ = -Math.ceil(maxChunkDistance); offsetZ <= Math.ceil(maxChunkDistance); offsetZ++) {
                    const address = createSliceAddress(
                        neighbor.sliceId, projectedAddress.chunkX + offsetX, projectedAddress.chunkZ + offsetZ
                    );
                    const reachability = this.getReachability(sourceAddress, address, {
                        ...options, position4D, maxChunkDistance, corridorId: neighbor.corridorId
                    });
                    if (reachability.reachable) {
                        candidates.set(getSliceAddressKey(address), { address, reachability });
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return Array.from(candidates.values());
    }

    getReachableAdjacentWorldChunks(address, position4D, options = {}) {
        const local = this.getNeighbors(address).filter((node) => node.kind === 'world');
        const remote = this.getReachableChunkCandidates(address.sliceId, position4D, options)
            .map(({ address: candidate }) => this.getChunk(candidate));
        return deduplicateNodes([...local, ...remote]);
    }

    getAdjacentWorldChunks(address) {
        const neighbors = this.getNeighbors(address);
        const result = neighbors.filter(function isLocalChunk(node) {
            return node.kind === 'world';
        });
        for (const corridorNode of neighbors.filter(function isCorridor(node) {
            return node.kind === 'corridor';
        })) {
            for (const destination of this.getNeighbors(corridorNode.key)) {
                if (destination.kind !== 'world' || sameAddress(destination.address, address)) continue;
                result.push({ ...destination, viaCorridorId: corridorNode.id });
            }
        }
        return deduplicateNodes(result);
    }

    getNeighborsOfCorridor(corridorId) {
        return this.getNeighbors(this.getCorridorKey(corridorId));
    }

    getRoute(fromAddress, toAddress, maxNodes = 4096) {
        const start = getSliceAddressKey(fromAddress);
        const goal = getSliceAddressKey(toAddress);
        if (fromAddress.sliceId !== toAddress.sliceId &&
            !this.hasSliceRoute(fromAddress.sliceId, toAddress.sliceId)) return null;
        const queue = [start];
        const previous = new Map([[start, null]]);
        while (queue.length && previous.size <= maxNodes) {
            const current = queue.shift();
            if (current === goal) break;
            for (const node of this.getNeighbors(current)) {
                const next = node.key;
                if (previous.has(next)) continue;
                previous.set(next, current);
                queue.push(next);
            }
        }
        if (!previous.has(goal)) return null;
        const route = [];
        for (let key = goal; key !== null; key = previous.get(key)) route.unshift(this.describeNode(key));
        return route;
    }

    hasSliceRoute(fromSliceId, toSliceId) {
        if (fromSliceId === toSliceId) return true;
        const visited = new Set([fromSliceId]);
        const queue = [fromSliceId];
        while (queue.length) {
            const current = queue.shift();
            for (const neighbor of this.topology.getTraversableNeighbors(current)) {
                if (neighbor.sliceId === toSliceId) return true;
                if (visited.has(neighbor.sliceId)) continue;
                visited.add(neighbor.sliceId);
                queue.push(neighbor.sliceId);
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    getStats() {
        let occupantCount = 0;
        for (const link of this.corridors.values()) occupantCount += link.chunk.occupants.size;
        return {
            slices: this.slices.size,
            loadedWorldChunks: this.loadedChunks.size,
            corridorChunks: this.corridors.size,
            corridorOccupants: occupantCount
        };
    }

    describeWorldChunk(address, key) {
        const chunk = this.loadedChunks.get(key) ?? null;
        return { key, id: key, kind: 'world', address: { ...address }, loaded: Boolean(chunk), chunk };
    }

    describeNode(key, edge = null) {
        const link = Array.from(this.corridors.values()).find(function findCorridor(entry) {
            return entry.nodeKey === key;
        });
        if (link) {
            return {
                key, id: link.id, kind: 'corridor', chunk: link.chunk,
                traversable: link.chunk.corridor.traversable,
                direction: edge?.direction ?? 0,
                corridorId: link.id
            };
        }
        const address = parseAddressKey(key);
        return this.describeWorldChunk(address, key);
    }

    addEdge(from, to, corridorId, direction) {
        const list = this.edges.get(from) ?? [];
        list.push({ to, corridorId, direction });
        this.edges.set(from, list);
    }

    removeEdges(key, corridorId) {
        const list = (this.edges.get(key) ?? []).filter(function keepOtherEdge(edge) {
            return edge.corridorId !== corridorId;
        });
        if (list.length) this.edges.set(key, list);
        else this.edges.delete(key);
    }

    refreshLoadedChunkNeighbors() {
        for (const chunk of this.loadedChunks.values()) {
            if (typeof chunk.setSpatialNeighbors !== 'function') continue;
            chunk.setSpatialNeighbors(
                this.getNeighbors(chunk.address),
                this.getAdjacentWorldChunks(chunk.address)
            );
        }
    }

    getCorridorKey(corridorId) { return `corridor:${corridorId}`; }

    requireSlice(sliceId) {
        const slice = this.slices.get(sliceId);
        if (!slice) throw new Error(`Chunk graph has no registered slice: ${sliceId}`);
        return slice;
    }

    assertActive() {
        if (this.disposed) throw new Error('SpatialChunkGraph has been disposed.');
    }

    dispose() {
        if (this.disposed) return;
        for (const link of this.corridors.values()) link.chunk.dispose();
        this.corridors.clear();
        this.edges.clear();
        this.loadedChunks.clear();
        this.slices.clear();
        this.disposed = true;
    }
}

function unreachableResult(reason, sourceAddress, targetAddress, anomalyId = null) {
    return {
        reachable: false, spatialReachable: false, wReachable: false,
        spatialDistance: Infinity, spatialReach: 0, wDistance: Infinity, wReach: 0,
        kind: 'cross-slice', reason, sourceAddress, targetAddress, anomalyId, freedom: 0
    };
}

function parseAddressKey(key) {
    const [sliceId, chunkX, chunkZ] = JSON.parse(key);
    return createSliceAddress(sliceId, chunkX, chunkZ);
}

function sameAddress(left, right) {
    return left.sliceId === right.sliceId && left.chunkX === right.chunkX && left.chunkZ === right.chunkZ;
}

function deduplicateNodes(nodes) {
    const unique = new Map();
    for (const node of nodes) unique.set(node.key, node);
    return Array.from(unique.values());
}

export { SpatialChunkGraph };


========================================================================
FILE: src/world/terrain-color.js
========================================================================

/**
 * Shared terrain surface-color model.
 *
 * Soil classification is derived from normalized topographic elevation until
 * a dedicated world soil-distribution texture becomes available. Both the
 * worker and synchronous chunk renderer use this module so their palettes
 * cannot drift apart.
 */

const DEFAULT_SEED = 91827;

const SOIL_COLORS = Object.freeze({
    clay: [0.48, 0.29, 0.21],
    sand: [0.92, 0.73, 0.39],
    fertileSoil: [0.43, 0.29, 0.18],
    loam: [0.35, 0.25, 0.16],
    organic: [0.19, 0.13, 0.085],
    scree: [0.43, 0.41, 0.37]
});

const BEDROCK_COLORS = Object.freeze({
    limestone: [0.63, 0.60, 0.51],
    sandstone: [0.65, 0.43, 0.29],
    basalt: [0.24, 0.23, 0.22],
    granite: [0.39, 0.37, 0.34]
});

const SNOW_COLOR = [0.82, 0.84, 0.82];

/** Clamp a numeric value, defaulting to the normalized [0, 1] range. */
export function clamp(value, min = 0, max = 1) {
    if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {
        return value === Infinity ? max : min;
    }

    return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));
}

function lerp(start, end, amount) {
    return start + (end - start) * amount;
}

function hashNoise2D(x, z, seed = DEFAULT_SEED) {
    let value =
        Math.imul(x | 0, 374761393) +
        Math.imul(z | 0, 668265263) +
        Math.imul(seed | 0, 1442695041);

    value = Math.imul(
        value ^ (value >>> 13),
        1274126177
    );

    value ^= value >>> 16;

    return (value >>> 0) / 4294967295;
}

function smoothNoise2D(x, z, seed = DEFAULT_SEED) {
    const integerX = Math.floor(x);
    const integerZ = Math.floor(z);
    const fractionX = x - integerX;
    const fractionZ = z - integerZ;

    const blendX = fractionX * fractionX * (3 - 2 * fractionX);
    const blendZ = fractionZ * fractionZ * (3 - 2 * fractionZ);

    const topLeft = hashNoise2D(integerX, integerZ, seed);
    const topRight = hashNoise2D(integerX + 1, integerZ, seed);
    const bottomLeft = hashNoise2D(integerX, integerZ + 1, seed);
    const bottomRight = hashNoise2D(integerX + 1, integerZ + 1, seed);

    return lerp(
        lerp(topLeft, topRight, blendX),
        lerp(bottomLeft, bottomRight, blendX),
        blendZ
    );
}

function fractalNoise2D(x, z, seed, octaves = 3) {
    let value = 0;
    let amplitude = 1;
    let frequency = 1;
    let totalAmplitude = 0;

    for (let octave = 0; octave < octaves; octave++) {
        value += smoothNoise2D(
            x * frequency,
            z * frequency,
            seed + octave * 1013
        ) * amplitude;

        totalAmplitude += amplitude;
        amplitude *= 0.5;
        frequency *= 2;
    }

    return totalAmplitude > 0 ? value / totalAmplitude : 0;
}

function mixColor(first, second, amount) {
    const blend = clamp(amount);

    return [
        lerp(first[0], second[0], blend),
        lerp(first[1], second[1], blend),
        lerp(first[2], second[2], blend)
    ];
}

/**
 * Map normalized elevation to the current provisional soil distribution.
 * Values come from the grayscale topographic heightmap, not a soil texture.
 */
export function classifySoilType(elevation) {
    const value = clamp(elevation);

    if (value < 0.18) return "clay";
    if (value < 0.38) return "sand";
    if (value < 0.58) return "fertileSoil";
    if (value < 0.76) return "loam";
    if (value < 0.90) return "organic";

    return "scree";
}

function getBedrockColor(geology, elevation) {
    if (elevation > 0.70 && geology > 0.65) {
        return BEDROCK_COLORS.granite;
    }

    if (geology < 0.28) return BEDROCK_COLORS.limestone;
    if (geology < 0.48) return BEDROCK_COLORS.sandstone;
    if (geology > 0.84) return BEDROCK_COLORS.basalt;

    return BEDROCK_COLORS.granite;
}

function applyTextureVariation(color, variation) {
    const texture = 0.92 + variation * 0.16;
    const result = new Array(3);

    for (let channel = 0; channel < 3; channel++) {
        result[channel] = clamp(color[channel] * texture);
    }

    return result;
}

function applySnow(color, elevation) {
    if (elevation <= 0.92) return color;

    const snowBlend = clamp((elevation - 0.92) / 0.08);
    return mixColor(color, SNOW_COLOR, snowBlend);
}

/**
 * Return an RGB vertex color for a terrain sample.
 *
 * `height` is expressed in world units; `slope` is the horizontal gradient
 * magnitude. Invalid sample values are safely normalized instead of
 * propagating NaN values into Babylon.js vertex buffers.
 */
export function getTerrainVertexColor(
    height,
    slope,
    worldX,
    worldZ,
    minHeight,
    maxHeight,
    seed = DEFAULT_SEED
) {
    const heightRange = maxHeight - minHeight;
    const elevation = heightRange > 0
        ? clamp((height - minHeight) / heightRange)
        : 0;

    const safeSlope = Number.isFinite(slope) ? Math.max(0, slope) : 0;
    const safeX = Number.isFinite(worldX) ? worldX : 0;
    const safeZ = Number.isFinite(worldZ) ? worldZ : 0;
    const slopeDegrees = Math.atan(safeSlope) * 180 / Math.PI;

    const moisture =
        fractalNoise2D(safeX * 0.0025, safeZ * 0.0025, seed + 1000, 3) * 0.7 +
        fractalNoise2D(safeX * 0.009, safeZ * 0.009, seed + 3000, 3) * 0.3;

    const geology = fractalNoise2D(
        safeX * 0.00375,
        safeZ * 0.00375,
        seed + 2000,
        3
    );

    const variation = fractalNoise2D(
        safeX * 0.025,
        safeZ * 0.025,
        seed + 5000,
        3
    );

    const exposure = clamp(
        slopeDegrees / 42 * 0.78 +
        elevation * 0.12 +
        (1 - moisture) * 0.10
    );

    const soilType = classifySoilType(elevation);
    const soilColor = SOIL_COLORS[soilType];
    const bedrockColor = getBedrockColor(geology, elevation);
    const bedrockBlend = clamp((exposure - 0.25) / 0.65);

    const exposedColor = mixColor(
        soilColor,
        bedrockColor,
        bedrockBlend
    );

    const variedColor = applyTextureVariation(
        exposedColor,
        variation
    );

    const finalColor = applySnow(variedColor, elevation);

    return [
        clamp(finalColor[0]),
        clamp(finalColor[1]),
        clamp(finalColor[2])
    ];
}


========================================================================
FILE: src/world/terrain-mesh-worker.js
========================================================================

import { clamp, getTerrainVertexColor } from "./terrain-color.js";

let terrain = null;

function lerp(start, end, amount) {
    return start + (end - start) * amount;
}

function pixelValue(x, z) {
    const source = terrain;
    const pixelX = clamp(Math.floor(x), 0, source.width - 1);
    const pixelZ = clamp(Math.floor(z), 0, source.height - 1);
    const index = (pixelZ * source.width + pixelX) * 4;
    const red = source.pixels[index];
    const green = source.pixels[index + 1];
    const blue = source.pixels[index + 2];

    switch (source.heightChannel) {
        case "red":
            return red / 255;
        case "green":
            return green / 255;
        case "blue":
            return blue / 255;
        case "luminance":
            return (
                0.2126 * red +
                0.7152 * green +
                0.0722 * blue
            ) / 255;
        case "average":
        default:
            return (red + green + blue) / 765;
    }
}

function heightAt(worldX, worldZ) {
    const normalizedX = clamp(
        (worldX - terrain.minX) / terrain.worldSize
    );
    const normalizedZ = clamp(
        (worldZ - terrain.minZ) / terrain.worldSize
    );

    const pixelX = normalizedX * (terrain.width - 1);
    const pixelZ = normalizedZ * (terrain.height - 1);
    const x0 = Math.floor(pixelX);
    const z0 = Math.floor(pixelZ);
    const x1 = Math.min(x0 + 1, terrain.width - 1);
    const z1 = Math.min(z0 + 1, terrain.height - 1);
    const fractionX = pixelX - x0;
    const fractionZ = pixelZ - z0;

    const top = lerp(
        pixelValue(x0, z0),
        pixelValue(x1, z0),
        fractionX
    );
    const bottom = lerp(
        pixelValue(x0, z1),
        pixelValue(x1, z1),
        fractionX
    );
    const normalizedHeight = lerp(top, bottom, fractionZ);

    return lerp(
        terrain.minHeight,
        terrain.maxHeight,
        normalizedHeight
    );
}

function terrainColor(height, slope, worldX, worldZ) {
    return getTerrainVertexColor(
        height,
        slope,
        worldX,
        worldZ,
        terrain.minHeight,
        terrain.maxHeight
    );
}

function getMeshResolution(lod) {
    const resolutions = [32, 16, 8, 4];
    return resolutions[clamp(lod, 0, resolutions.length - 1)];
}

function createMeshBuffers(resolution) {
    const vertexCount = (resolution + 1) ** 2;
    const indexCount = resolution * resolution * 6;

    return {
        positions: new Float32Array(vertexCount * 3),
        normals: new Float32Array(vertexCount * 3),
        colors: new Float32Array(vertexCount * 4),
        indices: new Uint32Array(indexCount)
    };
}

function getEdgeHeight(edge, index, context) {
    const {
        originX,
        originZ,
        size,
        stride,
        lod,
        neighborLODs
    } = context;
    const otherLOD = neighborLODs[edge] ?? lod;
    const worldDistance = index * stride;

    if (otherLOD <= lod) {
        return sampleEdgeHeight(edge, worldDistance, context);
    }

    const coarseStride = size / getMeshResolution(otherLOD);
    const lower = Math.floor(worldDistance / coarseStride) * coarseStride;
    const upper = Math.min(lower + coarseStride, size);
    const blend = upper === lower
        ? 0
        : (worldDistance - lower) / (upper - lower);

    const lowerPosition = getEdgePosition(edge, lower, context);
    const upperPosition = getEdgePosition(edge, upper, context);

    return lerp(
        heightAt(lowerPosition.x, lowerPosition.z),
        heightAt(upperPosition.x, upperPosition.z),
        blend
    );
}

function getEdgePosition(edge, distance, context) {
    const { originX, originZ, size } = context;

    if (edge === "north" || edge === "south") {
        return {
            x: originX + distance,
            z: originZ + (edge === "south" ? size : 0)
        };
    }

    return {
        x: originX + (edge === "east" ? size : 0),
        z: originZ + distance
    };
}

function sampleEdgeHeight(edge, distance, context) {
    const position = getEdgePosition(edge, distance, context);
    return heightAt(position.x, position.z);
}

function writeTerrainVertex(ix, iz, context, buffers) {
    const sample = getTerrainVertexSample(ix, iz, context);
    const vertexIndex = iz * (context.resolution + 1) + ix;

    writeVertexPositionAndNormal(sample, vertexIndex, buffers);
    writeVertexColor(sample, vertexIndex, buffers);
}

function getTerrainVertexSample(ix, iz, context) {
    const worldX = context.originX + ix * context.stride;
    const worldZ = context.originZ + iz * context.stride;
    const height = getVertexHeight(ix, iz, worldX, worldZ, context);
    const spacing = Math.max(2, context.stride * 0.25);
    const gradientX = getHeightGradientX(worldX, worldZ, spacing);
    const gradientZ = getHeightGradientZ(worldX, worldZ, spacing);

    return {
        worldX,
        worldZ,
        height,
        gradientX,
        gradientZ,
        normalLength: Math.hypot(gradientX, 1, gradientZ),
        slope: Math.hypot(gradientX, gradientZ)
    };
}

function getHeightGradientX(worldX, worldZ, spacing) {
    return (
        heightAt(worldX + spacing, worldZ) -
        heightAt(worldX - spacing, worldZ)
    ) / (2 * spacing);
}

function getHeightGradientZ(worldX, worldZ, spacing) {
    return (
        heightAt(worldX, worldZ + spacing) -
        heightAt(worldX, worldZ - spacing)
    ) / (2 * spacing);
}

function writeVertexPositionAndNormal(sample, vertexIndex, buffers) {
    const offset = vertexIndex * 3;

    buffers.positions[offset] = sample.worldX;
    buffers.positions[offset + 1] = sample.height;
    buffers.positions[offset + 2] = sample.worldZ;

    buffers.normals[offset] = -sample.gradientX / sample.normalLength;
    buffers.normals[offset + 1] = 1 / sample.normalLength;
    buffers.normals[offset + 2] = -sample.gradientZ / sample.normalLength;
}

function writeVertexColor(sample, vertexIndex, buffers) {
    const offset = vertexIndex * 4;
    const color = terrainColor(
        sample.height,
        sample.slope,
        sample.worldX,
        sample.worldZ
    );

    buffers.colors[offset] = color[0];
    buffers.colors[offset + 1] = color[1];
    buffers.colors[offset + 2] = color[2];
    buffers.colors[offset + 3] = 1;
}

function getVertexHeight(ix, iz, worldX, worldZ, context) {
    const { resolution } = context;
    let height = heightAt(worldX, worldZ);

    if (iz === 0) {
        height = getEdgeHeight("north", ix, context);
    }

    if (iz === resolution) {
        height = getEdgeHeight("south", ix, context);
    }

    if (ix === 0) {
        height = getEdgeHeight("west", iz, context);
    }

    if (ix === resolution) {
        height = getEdgeHeight("east", iz, context);
    }

    return height;
}

function writeMeshIndices(resolution, indices) {
    let cursor = 0;

    for (let iz = 0; iz < resolution; iz++) {
        for (let ix = 0; ix < resolution; ix++) {
            const topLeft = iz * (resolution + 1) + ix;
            const topRight = topLeft + 1;
            const bottomLeft = topLeft + resolution + 1;
            const bottomRight = bottomLeft + 1;

            indices[cursor++] = topLeft;
            indices[cursor++] = bottomLeft;
            indices[cursor++] = topRight;
            indices[cursor++] = topRight;
            indices[cursor++] = bottomLeft;
            indices[cursor++] = bottomRight;
        }
    }
}

function generateMesh(job) {
    const resolution = getMeshResolution(job.lod);
    const stride = job.size / resolution;
    const buffers = createMeshBuffers(resolution);
    const context = {
        resolution,
        stride,
        size: job.size,
        originX: job.x * job.size,
        originZ: job.z * job.size,
        lod: job.lod,
        neighborLODs: job.neighborLODs ?? {}
    };

    for (let iz = 0; iz <= resolution; iz++) {
        for (let ix = 0; ix <= resolution; ix++) {
            writeTerrainVertex(ix, iz, context, buffers);
        }
    }

    writeMeshIndices(resolution, buffers.indices);
    validateGeneratedColors(buffers.colors);

    return buffers;
}

function validateWorkerTerrain(message) {
    const settings = message.terrain;
    const pixels = new Uint8ClampedArray(message.pixels);
    const expectedLength = settings.width * settings.height * 4;

    if (
        !Number.isInteger(settings.width) || settings.width < 1 ||
        !Number.isInteger(settings.height) || settings.height < 1 ||
        !Number.isFinite(settings.worldSize) || settings.worldSize <= 0 ||
        !Number.isFinite(settings.minX) ||
        !Number.isFinite(settings.minZ) ||
        !Number.isFinite(settings.minHeight) ||
        !Number.isFinite(settings.maxHeight) ||
        settings.maxHeight <= settings.minHeight ||
        pixels.length < expectedLength
    ) {
        throw new Error("Terrain worker received invalid heightmap data.");
    }

    terrain = {
        ...settings,
        pixels
    };
}

function validateGeneratedColors(colors) {
    for (let index = 0; index < colors.length; index++) {
        if (!Number.isFinite(colors[index])) {
            throw new Error(
                `Terrain color generation produced a non-finite value at ${index}.`
            );
        }
    }
}

function transferMeshData(message, buffers) {
    self.postMessage({
        type: "complete",
        id: message.id,
        revision: message.revision,
        data: buffers
    }, [
        buffers.positions.buffer,
        buffers.normals.buffer,
        buffers.colors.buffer,
        buffers.indices.buffer
    ]);
}

function postWorkerError(message, error) {
    self.postMessage({
        type: "error",
        id: message.id,
        revision: message.revision,
        message: error.message
    });
}

function handleWorkerMessage(event) {
    const message = event.data;

    if (message.type === "init") {
        validateWorkerTerrain(message);
        self.postMessage({ type: "ready" });
        return;
    }

    if (message.type !== "generate" || !terrain) return;

    try {
        const buffers = generateMesh(message.job);
        transferMeshData(message, buffers);
    } catch (error) {
        postWorkerError(message, error);
    }
}

self.onmessage = handleWorkerMessage;


========================================================================
FILE: src/world/topographic-world-factory.js
========================================================================

/** Creates independent, serializable world slices from decoded topographic rasters. */

import { createHeightmapData, getHeightmapStatistics, sampleHeightmapData, sampleNormalizedHeightmapData } from '../environment/topography/heightmap-data.js';
import { createWorldSlice } from './fields/geometry/world-slice.js';

class TopographicWorldFactory {
    create(options = {}) {
        const map = createHeightmapData({
            ...(options.raster ?? {}),
            channel: options.heightChannel ?? options.raster?.channel ?? 'luminance',
            minHeight: options.minHeight ?? 0,
            maxHeight: options.maxHeight ?? 100
        });
        const worldSizeX = options.worldSizeX ?? options.worldSize ?? 1000;
        const worldSizeZ = options.worldSizeZ ?? options.worldSize ?? 1000;
        validateWorldSize(worldSizeX, worldSizeZ);

        const slice = createWorldSlice({
            id: options.id,
            w: options.w ?? 0,
            geometry: options.geometry ?? 'euclidean',
            seed: options.seed ?? 0,
            revision: options.revision ?? 1,
            frame: options.frame
        });
        const terrain = new TopographicTerrain(map, worldSizeX, worldSizeZ);
        const metadata = {
            source: options.source ?? null,
            width: map.width,
            height: map.height,
            channel: map.channel,
            colorStopCount: map.colorStops.length,
            minHeight: map.minHeight,
            maxHeight: map.maxHeight,
            statistics: getHeightmapStatistics(map, options.statisticsSamples ?? 24)
        };

        return { slice, terrain, metadata };
    }
}

class TopographicTerrain {
    constructor(map, worldSizeX, worldSizeZ) {
        this.map = map;
        this.pixels = map.pixels;
        this.width = map.width;
        this.height = map.height;
        this.worldSizeX = worldSizeX;
        this.worldSizeZ = worldSizeZ;
        this.worldSize = Math.max(worldSizeX, worldSizeZ);
        this.minHeight = map.minHeight;
        this.maxHeight = map.maxHeight;
        this.heightChannel = map.channel;
        this.min = { x: -worldSizeX / 2, y: map.minHeight, z: -worldSizeZ / 2 };
        this.max = { x: worldSizeX / 2, y: map.maxHeight, z: worldSizeZ / 2 };
        this.disposed = false;
    }

    get ready() { return !this.disposed; }

    sampleHeight(x, z) {
        this.assertReady();
        if (!this.contains(x, z)) return null;
        return sampleHeightmapData(this.map, this.toU(x), this.toV(z));
    }

    sampleAt(x, z) {
        const y = this.sampleHeight(x, z);
        return y === null ? null : { x, y, z };
    }

    sampleMapValue(x, z) {
        this.assertReady();
        if (!this.contains(x, z)) return null;
        return sampleNormalizedHeightmapData(this.map, this.toU(x), this.toV(z));
    }

    sampleSlopeAt(x, z, spacing = 1) {
        if (!Number.isFinite(spacing) || spacing <= 0) {
            throw new RangeError('Terrain slope spacing must be finite and positive.');
        }
        const left = this.sampleHeight(x - spacing, z);
        const right = this.sampleHeight(x + spacing, z);
        const back = this.sampleHeight(x, z - spacing);
        const front = this.sampleHeight(x, z + spacing);
        if ([left, right, back, front].some(isNull)) return null;
        const gradientX = (right - left) / (2 * spacing);
        const gradientZ = (front - back) / (2 * spacing);
        const magnitude = Math.hypot(gradientX, gradientZ);
        return {
            gradientX,
            gradientZ,
            magnitude,
            angle: Math.atan(magnitude),
            degrees: Math.atan(magnitude) * 180 / Math.PI,
            normal: normalize3({ x: -gradientX, y: 1, z: -gradientZ })
        };
    }

    sampleProfileAt(x, z, spacing = 1) {
        const position = this.sampleAt(x, z);
        const slope = this.sampleSlopeAt(x, z, spacing);
        if (!position || !slope) return null;
        return {
            position,
            elevation: clamp((position.y - this.minHeight) / (this.maxHeight - this.minHeight), 0, 1),
            slope: slope.magnitude,
            slopeDegrees: slope.degrees,
            normal: slope.normal
        };
    }

    getDiagnostics(samples = 24) {
        this.assertReady();
        return {
            ready: !this.disposed,
            width: this.width,
            height: this.height,
            worldSizeX: this.worldSizeX,
            worldSizeZ: this.worldSizeZ,
            heightChannel: this.heightChannel,
            sampledHeightRange: getHeightmapStatistics(this.map, samples)
        };
    }

    contains(x, z) {
        return Number.isFinite(x) && Number.isFinite(z) &&
            x >= this.min.x && x <= this.max.x && z >= this.min.z && z <= this.max.z;
    }

    toU(x) { return (x - this.min.x) / this.worldSizeX; }
    toV(z) { return (z - this.min.z) / this.worldSizeZ; }

    assertReady() {
        if (this.disposed) throw new Error('Topographic terrain has been disposed.');
    }

    dispose() {
        this.disposed = true;
        this.map = null;
        this.pixels = null;
    }
}

function validateWorldSize(width, depth) {
    if (!Number.isFinite(width) || width <= 0 || !Number.isFinite(depth) || depth <= 0) {
        throw new RangeError('Topographic world dimensions must be finite and positive.');
    }
}

function normalize3(vector) {
    const length = Math.hypot(vector.x, vector.y, vector.z);
    return { x: vector.x / length, y: vector.y / length, z: vector.z / length };
}

function clamp(value, minimum, maximum) {
    return Math.min(maximum, Math.max(minimum, value));
}

function isNull(value) { return value === null; }

export { TopographicTerrain, TopographicWorldFactory };

FILE: src/world/world-chunk.js

import { getTerrainVertexColor } from "./terrain-color.js";
import { createSliceAddress } from "./fields/geometry/world-slice.js";

export class WorldChunk {
    constructor(x, z, size, terrain, scene, options = {}) {
        this.x = x;
        this.z = z;
        this.size = size;
        this.terrain = terrain;
        this.scene = scene;
        this.sliceId = options.sliceId ?? 'somnium-main';
        this.address = createSliceAddress(this.sliceId, x, z);
        this.spatialAdapter = options.spatialAdapter ?? null;
        this.opticalScene = options.opticalScene ?? this.spatialAdapter?.opticalScene ?? null;
        this.opticalRegistrationId = null;
        this.layerMask = options.layerMask;
        this.visible = options.visible ?? true;
        this.spatialNeighbors = [];
        this.adjacentWorldChunks = [];

        this.mesh = null;
        this.lod = -1;
        this.loaded = false;
        this.disposed = false;
        this.revision = 0;

        this.neighbors = {
            north: null,
            south: null,
            east: null,
            west: null
        };
    }

    get originX() {
        return this.x * this.size;
    }

    get originZ() {
        return this.z * this.size;
    }

    getResolution(lod) {
        const resolutions = [32, 16, 8, 4];
        const index = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(lod, 0, 3);
        return resolutions[index];
    }

    setNeighbors(neighbors) {
        this.neighbors = neighbors;
    }

    setSpatialNeighbors(neighbors, worldChunks = []) {
        this.spatialNeighbors = Array.isArray(neighbors) ? neighbors : [];
        this.adjacentWorldChunks = Array.isArray(worldChunks) ? worldChunks : [];
    }

    validateMeshData(data) {
        if (!data || !data.positions || !data.normals || !data.colors || !data.indices) {
            throw new Error("Terrain mesh data is incomplete.");
        }

        const vertexCount = data.positions.length / 3;

        if (
            !Number.isInteger(vertexCount) ||
            data.normals.length !== vertexCount * 3 ||
            data.colors.length !== vertexCount * 4
        ) {
            throw new Error("Terrain mesh vertex buffers have inconsistent lengths.");
        }

        for (let index = 0; index < data.colors.length; index++) {
            if (!Number.isFinite(data.colors[index])) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Terrain mesh contains a non-finite vertex color at ${index}.`
                );
            }
        }
    }

    applyMeshData(data, lod, material) {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        this.validateMeshData(data);

        const vertexData = new BABYLON.VertexData();
        vertexData.positions = Array.from(data.positions);
        vertexData.normals = Array.from(data.normals);
        vertexData.colors = Array.from(data.colors);
        vertexData.indices = Array.from(data.indices);

        this.disposeMesh();
        this.mesh = new BABYLON.Mesh(
            `terrain_chunk_${this.sliceId}_${this.x}_${this.z}`,
            this.scene
        );

        vertexData.applyToMesh(this.mesh, true);
        this.mesh.isVisible = this.visible;
        if (Number.isInteger(this.layerMask)) this.mesh.layerMask = this.layerMask;
        this.configureMeshMaterial(material);
        this.configureMeshPicking(lod);
        this.registerOpticalHeightfield();
        this.applySpatialTransform();

        this.lod = lod;
        this.loaded = true;
    }

    registerOpticalHeightfield() {
        if (!this.opticalScene || typeof this.terrain?.sampleHeight !== 'function') return;
        this.opticalRegistrationId = `world-heightfield:${this.sliceId}:${this.x}:${this.z}`;
        this.opticalScene.registerHeightfield({
            id: this.opticalRegistrationId,
            sliceId: this.sliceId,
            chunkAddress: this.address,
            terrain: this.terrain,
            bounds: {
                minX: this.originX,
                maxX: this.originX + this.size,
                minZ: this.originZ,
                maxZ: this.originZ + this.size
            },
            revision: Math.max(1, this.revision)
        });
        this.mesh.metadata = { ...(this.mesh.metadata || {}), opticalIgnore: true, opticalHeightfieldId: this.opticalRegistrationId };
    }

    disposeMesh() {
        if (!this.mesh) {
            if (this.opticalRegistrationId) this.opticalScene?.unregister(this.opticalRegistrationId);
            this.opticalRegistrationId = null;
            return;
        }

        if (this.opticalRegistrationId) this.opticalScene?.unregister(this.opticalRegistrationId);
        this.opticalRegistrationId = null;
        this.spatialAdapter?.restoreMesh(this.mesh);
        this.mesh.dispose();
        this.mesh = null;
    }

    configureMeshMaterial(material) {
        this.mesh.material = material;
        material.useVertexColors = true;
        material.diffuseColor.set(1, 1, 1);
        this.mesh.useVertexColors = true;
        this.mesh.hasVertexAlpha = false;
    }

    configureMeshPicking(lod) {
        this.mesh.isPickable = true;
        this.mesh.metadata = {
            sliceId: this.sliceId,
            chunkX: this.x,
            chunkZ: this.z,
            lod,
            terrainChunk: true
        };

        if (!this.mesh.isVerticesDataPresent(BABYLON.VertexBuffer.ColorKind)) {
            throw new Error(
                `[WorldChunk] Terrain vertex colors missing for ${this.mesh.name}.`
            );
        }
    }

    applySpatialTransform() {
        if (this.spatialAdapter) this.spatialAdapter.applyMesh(this.mesh, this.sliceId);
        else this.scene.metadata?.geometryRenderer?.applyMesh(this.mesh);
    }

    generateMesh(lod, material) {
        if (this.disposed) return;

        const resolution = this.getResolution(lod);
        const buffers = this.createMeshBuffers(resolution);

        for (let z = 0; z <= resolution; z++) {
            for (let x = 0; x <= resolution; x++) {
                this.writeVertex(x, z, resolution, buffers);
            }
        }

        this.writeIndices(resolution, buffers.indices);
        this.applyMeshData(buffers, lod, material);
    }

    createMeshBuffers(resolution) {
        const vertexCount = (resolution + 1) ** 2;
        const indexCount = resolution * resolution * 6;

        return {
            positions: new Float32Array(vertexCount * 3),
            normals: new Float32Array(vertexCount * 3),
            colors: new Float32Array(vertexCount * 4),
            indices: new Uint32Array(indexCount)
        };
    }

    getHeightAt(x, z) {
        const clampedX = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            x,
            this.terrain.min.x,
            this.terrain.max.x
        );
        const clampedZ = BABYLON.Scalar.Clamp(
            z,
            this.terrain.min.z,
            this.terrain.max.z
        );

        return this.terrain.sampleHeight(clampedX, clampedZ);
    }

    getTerrainColor(height, slope, worldX, worldZ) {
        return getTerrainVertexColor(
            height,
            slope,
            worldX,
            worldZ,
            this.terrain.minHeight,
            this.terrain.maxHeight
        );
    }

    writeVertex(x, z, resolution, buffers) {
        const sample = this.getVertexSample(x, z, resolution);
        const vertexIndex = z * (resolution + 1) + x;

        this.writeVertexPositionAndNormal(sample, vertexIndex, buffers);
        this.writeVertexColor(sample, vertexIndex, buffers);
    }

    getVertexSample(x, z, resolution) {
        const stride = this.size / resolution;
        const worldX = this.originX + x * stride;
        const worldZ = this.originZ + z * stride;
        const height = this.getHeightAt(worldX, worldZ);
        const slope = this.terrain.sampleSlopeAt(
            worldX,
            worldZ,
            Math.max(2, stride * 0.25)
        );
        const normal = slope?.normal ?? new BABYLON.Vector3(0, 1, 0);

        return {
            worldX,
            worldZ,
            height,
            slope: slope?.magnitude ?? 0,
            normal
        };
    }

    writeVertexPositionAndNormal(sample, vertexIndex, buffers) {
        const offset = vertexIndex * 3;

        buffers.positions[offset] = sample.worldX;
        buffers.positions[offset + 1] = sample.height;
        buffers.positions[offset + 2] = sample.worldZ;

        buffers.normals[offset] = sample.normal.x;
        buffers.normals[offset + 1] = sample.normal.y;
        buffers.normals[offset + 2] = sample.normal.z;
    }

    writeVertexColor(sample, vertexIndex, buffers) {
        const offset = vertexIndex * 4;
        const color = this.getTerrainColor(
            sample.height,
            sample.slope,
            sample.worldX,
            sample.worldZ
        );

        buffers.colors[offset] = color[0];
        buffers.colors[offset + 1] = color[1];
        buffers.colors[offset + 2] = color[2];
        buffers.colors[offset + 3] = 1;
    }

    writeIndices(resolution, indices) {
        let cursor = 0;

        for (let z = 0; z < resolution; z++) {
            for (let x = 0; x < resolution; x++) {
                const topLeft = z * (resolution + 1) + x;
                const topRight = topLeft + 1;
                const bottomLeft = topLeft + resolution + 1;
                const bottomRight = bottomLeft + 1;

                indices[cursor++] = topLeft;
                indices[cursor++] = bottomLeft;
                indices[cursor++] = topRight;
                indices[cursor++] = topRight;
                indices[cursor++] = bottomLeft;
                indices[cursor++] = bottomRight;
            }
        }
    }

    dispose() {
        this.revision++;
        this.disposeMesh();
        this.loaded = false;
        this.disposed = true;
    }
}

FILE: src/world/world-manager.js

import { WorldChunk } from "./world-chunk.js?v=somnium-20261109-phase17i-bidirectional-spherical-passage-1";

export class WorldManager {
    constructor(scene, terrain, options = {}) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.terrain = terrain;
        this.sliceId = options.sliceId ?? 'somnium-main';
        this.chunkGraph = options.chunkGraph ?? null;
        this.spatialAdapter = options.spatialAdapter ?? null;
        this.opticalScene = options.opticalScene ?? this.spatialAdapter?.opticalScene ?? null;
        this.layerMask = options.layerMask;
        this.visible = options.visible ?? true;

	this.fallbackScheduled = false;

        this.chunkSize = options.chunkSize ?? 2000;
        if (this.chunkGraph && !this.chunkGraph.hasSlice(this.sliceId)) {
            this.chunkGraph.registerSlice(this.sliceId, { chunkSize: this.chunkSize });
        }
        this.renderRadius = options.renderRadius ?? 14;
        this.highDetailRadius = options.highDetailRadius ?? 2;

        this.maxChunksPerUpdate =
            options.maxChunksPerUpdate ?? 6;

        this.chunks = new Map();
        this.remoteChunkRetention = new Map();
        this.remoteRetentionFrames = options.remoteRetentionFrames ?? 30;
        this.lastReachableRemoteChunks = 0;

        this.enabled = true;

        this.stats = {
            generated: 0,
            unloaded: 0,
            loaded: 0,
            workerCompleted: 0,
            workerErrors: 0,
            pendingMeshes: 0
        };

        this.minChunkX = Math.floor(
            terrain.min.x / this.chunkSize
        );

        this.maxChunkX = Math.ceil(
            terrain.max.x / this.chunkSize
        ) - 1;

        this.minChunkZ = Math.floor(
            terrain.min.z / this.chunkSize
        );

        this.maxChunkZ = Math.ceil(
            terrain.max.z / this.chunkSize
        ) - 1;

this.material = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial(
    "worldTerrainMaterial",
    scene
);

this.material.diffuseColor = new BABYLON.Color3(
    1.0, 1.0, 1.0
);

this.material.ambientColor = new BABYLON.Color3(
    1, 1, 1
);

// Preserve vertex colors, but allow actual illumination.
this.material.useVertexColors = true;
this.material.disableLighting = false;

// Small nighttime visibility contribution.
this.material.emissiveColor = new BABYLON.Color3(
    0.1, 0.1, 0.1
);

this.material.backFaceCulling = false;

this.material.specularColor = BABYLON.Color3.Black();
this.material.roughness = 1.0;
	
        this.meshQueue = [];
        this.worker = null;
        this.workerReady = false;
        this.workerBusy = false;
        this.nextTaskId = 1;

        this.initializeWorker();
    }

    getKey(x, z) {
        return JSON.stringify([this.sliceId, x, z]);
    }

    getChunk(x, z) {
        return this.chunks.get(this.getKey(x, z)) ?? null;
    }

    getCoordinates(position) {
        return {
            x: Math.floor(position.x / this.chunkSize),
            z: Math.floor(position.z / this.chunkSize)
        };
    }

    isInsideWorld(x, z) {
        return (
            x >= this.minChunkX &&
            x <= this.maxChunkX &&
            z >= this.minChunkZ &&
            z <= this.maxChunkZ
        );
    }

	initializeWorker() {
    if (typeof Worker === "undefined") {
        console.warn(
            "[WorldManager] Workers unavailable. Using fallback."
        );
        return;
    }

    try {
        this.worker = new Worker(
            new URL("./terrain-mesh-worker.js", import.meta.url),
            { type: "module" }
        );

        this.worker.onmessage = event => {
            const message = event.data;

            if (message.type === "ready") {
                if (this.workerInitTimer) {
                    clearTimeout(this.workerInitTimer);
                    this.workerInitTimer = null;
                }

                this.workerReady = true;

                console.log(
                    "[WorldManager] Terrain worker ready."
                );

                this.pumpWorker();
                return;
            }

            this.handleWorkerMessage(message);
        };

        this.worker.onerror = error => {
            this.failWorker(error);
        };

        this.worker.onmessageerror = error => {
            this.failWorker(error);
        };

        const pixels = this.terrain.pixels.slice();

        this.worker.postMessage({
            type: "init",
            terrain: {
                width: this.terrain.width,
                height: this.terrain.height,
                worldSize: this.terrain.worldSize,
                minX: this.terrain.min.x,
                minZ: this.terrain.min.z,
                minHeight: this.terrain.minHeight,
                maxHeight: this.terrain.maxHeight,
                heightChannel:
                    this.terrain.heightChannel ?? "average"
            },
            pixels: pixels.buffer
        }, [pixels.buffer]);

        this.workerInitTimer = setTimeout(() => {
            if (!this.workerReady && this.worker) {
                this.failWorker(
                    "Worker initialization timed out."
                );
            }
        }, 5000);

    } catch (error) {
        this.failWorker(error);
    }
}

    handleWorkerMessage(message) {
        if (message.type === "ready") {
            this.workerReady = true;
            this.pumpWorker();
            return;
        }

        if (
            message.type === "complete" ||
            message.type === "error"
        ) {
            const task = this.currentTask;

            this.workerBusy = false;
            this.currentTask = null;

            if (
                task &&
                task.id === message.id
            ) {
                const chunk = this.chunks.get(task.key);

                if (
                    chunk &&
                    !chunk.disposed &&
                    chunk.revision === message.revision
                ) {
                    if (message.type === "complete") {
                        chunk.applyMeshData(
                            message.data,
                            task.lod,
                            this.material
                        );

                        this.stats.workerCompleted++;
                    } else {
                        this.stats.workerErrors++;

                        console.error(
                            "[WorldManager] Mesh generation failed:",
                            message.message
                        );

                        chunk.generateMesh(
                            task.lod,
                            this.material
                        );
                    }
                }
            }

            this.pumpWorker();
        }
    }

    scheduleMesh(chunk, lod) {
        if (!chunk || chunk.disposed) return;

        const neighbors = this.getNeighbors(chunk);

        chunk.setNeighbors(neighbors);
        chunk.lod = lod;
        chunk.revision++;

        const job = {
            id: this.nextTaskId++,
            key: this.getKey(chunk.x, chunk.z),
            revision: chunk.revision,
            lod,

            x: chunk.x,
            z: chunk.z,
            size: chunk.size,

            neighborLODs: {
                north: neighbors.north?.lod ?? lod,
                south: neighbors.south?.lod ?? lod,
                east: neighbors.east?.lod ?? lod,
                west: neighbors.west?.lod ?? lod
            }
        };

        this.meshQueue = this.meshQueue.filter(
            item => item.key !== job.key
        );

        this.meshQueue.push(job);

        this.stats.pendingMeshes = this.meshQueue.length;

        this.pumpWorker();
    }

    pumpWorker() {
        if (
            !this.worker ||
            !this.workerReady ||
            this.workerBusy ||
            this.meshQueue.length === 0
        ) {
            return;
        }

        const job = this.meshQueue.shift();

        this.stats.pendingMeshes = this.meshQueue.length;

        const chunk = this.chunks.get(job.key);

        if (
            !chunk ||
            chunk.disposed ||
            chunk.revision !== job.revision
        ) {
            this.pumpWorker();
            return;
        }

        this.currentTask = job;
        this.workerBusy = true;

        this.worker.postMessage({
            type: "generate",
            id: job.id,
            revision: job.revision,
            job
        });
    }

	processFallbackQueue() {
    if (
        this.fallbackScheduled ||
        this.meshQueue.length === 0 ||
        !this.enabled
    ) {
        return;
    }

    this.fallbackScheduled = true;

    setTimeout(() => {
        this.fallbackScheduled = false;

        const job = this.meshQueue.shift();

        if (job) {
            const chunk = this.chunks.get(job.key);

            if (
                chunk &&
                !chunk.disposed &&
                chunk.revision === job.revision
            ) {
                try {
                    chunk.generateMesh(
                        job.lod,
                        this.material
                    );
                } catch (error) {
                    console.error(
                        "[WorldManager] Synchronous terrain generation failed:",
                        error
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        this.stats.pendingMeshes = this.meshQueue.length;
        this.processFallbackQueue();

    }, 0);
}

    getNeighbors(chunk) {
        return {
            north: this.getChunk(chunk.x, chunk.z - 1),
            south: this.getChunk(chunk.x, chunk.z + 1),
            east: this.getChunk(chunk.x + 1, chunk.z),
            west: this.getChunk(chunk.x - 1, chunk.z)
        };
    }

    getSpatialNeighbors(chunk, position4D = null) {
        if (!this.chunkGraph) return [];
        const neighbors = this.chunkGraph.getNeighbors(chunk.address);
        const worldChunks = position4D && this.chunkGraph.getReachableAdjacentWorldChunks
            ? this.chunkGraph.getReachableAdjacentWorldChunks(chunk.address, position4D)
            : this.chunkGraph.getAdjacentWorldChunks(chunk.address);
        chunk.setSpatialNeighbors(neighbors, worldChunks);
        return neighbors;
    }

    rebuildChunk(chunk) {
        this.scheduleMesh(
            chunk,
            chunk.lod < 0 ? 3 : chunk.lod
        );
    }

    createChunk(x, z, lod) {
        if (!this.isInsideWorld(x, z)) return null;

        const chunk = new WorldChunk(
            x,
            z,
            this.chunkSize,
            this.terrain,
            this.scene,
            {
                sliceId: this.sliceId,
                spatialAdapter: this.spatialAdapter,
                opticalScene: this.opticalScene,
                layerMask: this.layerMask,
                visible: this.visible
            }
        );

        this.chunks.set(
            this.getKey(x, z),
            chunk
        );
        this.chunkGraph?.registerChunk(chunk.address, chunk);
        chunk.setSpatialNeighbors(this.getSpatialNeighbors(chunk));

        this.stats.generated++;
        this.stats.loaded++;

        this.scheduleMesh(chunk, lod);

        // Existing neighbors may need new boundary stitching.
        for (const neighbor of Object.values(
            this.getNeighbors(chunk)
        )) {
            if (neighbor) {
                this.rebuildChunk(neighbor);
            }
        }

        return chunk;
    }

    getLOD(x, z, cameraPosition) {
        const centerX = (x + 0.5) * this.chunkSize;
        const centerZ = (z + 0.5) * this.chunkSize;

        const distance = Math.hypot(
            centerX - cameraPosition.x,
            centerZ - cameraPosition.z
        );

if (
    distance <=
    this.chunkSize * (this.highDetailRadius + 0.5)
) return 0;

if (
    distance <=
    this.chunkSize * (this.highDetailRadius + 2)
) return 1;

if (distance <= this.chunkSize * 8) return 2;

        return 3;
    }

    getPriority(x, z, cameraPosition, forward) {
        const centerX = (x + 0.5) * this.chunkSize;
        const centerZ = (z + 0.5) * this.chunkSize;

        const dx = centerX - cameraPosition.x;
        const dz = centerZ - cameraPosition.z;

        const distance = Math.hypot(dx, dz);

        const length = Math.max(distance, 1);

        const directionDot = (
            dx / length * forward.x +
            dz / length * forward.z
        );

        // Lower scores are generated first.
        return distance -
            Math.max(0, directionDot) *
            this.chunkSize * 4;
    }

    update(cameraPosition, forward = null, reachabilityContext = null) {
        if (!this.enabled || !this.terrain.ready) return;

        const center = this.getCoordinates(cameraPosition);

        const direction = forward ?? {
            x: 0,
            z: 1
        };

        const required = [];
        const requiredKeys = new Set();

        for (
            let x = Math.max(
                this.minChunkX,
                center.x - this.renderRadius
            );
            x <= Math.min(
                this.maxChunkX,
                center.x + this.renderRadius
            );
            x++
        ) {
            for (
                let z = Math.max(
                    this.minChunkZ,
                    center.z - this.renderRadius
                );
                z <= Math.min(
                    this.maxChunkZ,
                    center.z + this.renderRadius
                );
                z++
            ) {
                const dx = x - center.x;
                const dz = z - center.z;

                if (
                    dx * dx + dz * dz >
                    this.renderRadius * this.renderRadius
                ) continue;

                required.push({
                    x,
                    z,
                    priority: this.getPriority(
                        x,
                        z,
                        cameraPosition,
                        direction
                    ),
                    lod: this.getLOD(x, z, cameraPosition)
                });
                requiredKeys.add(`${x}:${z}`);
            }
        }

        this.lastReachableRemoteChunks = 0;
        const currentlyReachableKeys = new Set();
        if (this.chunkGraph?.getReachableChunkCandidates && reachabilityContext?.sourceSliceId &&
            reachabilityContext.sourceSliceId !== this.sliceId && reachabilityContext.position4D) {
            const candidates = this.chunkGraph.getReachableChunkCandidates(
                reachabilityContext.sourceSliceId, reachabilityContext.position4D,
                { ...(reachabilityContext.influenceOptions ?? {}), targetSliceId: this.sliceId }
            );
            for (const candidate of candidates) {
                const address = candidate.address;
                if (!this.isInsideWorld(address.chunkX, address.chunkZ)) continue;
                const key = `${address.chunkX}:${address.chunkZ}`;
                currentlyReachableKeys.add(key);
                this.remoteChunkRetention.set(key, this.remoteRetentionFrames);
                this.lastReachableRemoteChunks++;
                if (requiredKeys.has(key)) continue;
                required.push({
                    x: address.chunkX,
                    z: address.chunkZ,
                    priority: -1e6 + candidate.reachability.spatialDistance * this.chunkSize +
                        candidate.reachability.wDistance,
                    lod: this.getLOD(
                        address.chunkX, address.chunkZ,
                        candidate.reachability.projectedPosition ?? cameraPosition
                    ),
                    reachableViaAnomaly: candidate.reachability.anomalyId
                });
                requiredKeys.add(key);
            }
        }
        for (const [key, frames] of this.remoteChunkRetention) {
            if (currentlyReachableKeys.has(key)) continue;
            if (frames <= 1) this.remoteChunkRetention.delete(key);
            else this.remoteChunkRetention.set(key, frames - 1);
        }

        required.sort((a, b) => a.priority - b.priority);

        let scheduled = 0;

        for (const item of required) {
            const chunk = this.getChunk(item.x, item.z);

            if (!chunk) {
                if (scheduled >= this.maxChunksPerUpdate) {
                    continue;
                }

                this.createChunk(
                    item.x,
                    item.z,
                    item.lod
                );

                scheduled++;
                continue;
            }

            if (chunk.lod !== item.lod) {
                if (scheduled >= this.maxChunksPerUpdate) {
                    continue;
                }

                this.scheduleMesh(chunk, item.lod);
                scheduled++;
            }
        }

        const unloadRadius = this.renderRadius + 1;

        for (const [key, chunk] of this.chunks) {
            const dx = chunk.x - center.x;
            const dz = chunk.z - center.z;

            if (
                dx * dx + dz * dz > unloadRadius * unloadRadius &&
                (this.remoteChunkRetention.get(`${chunk.x}:${chunk.z}`) ?? 0) <= 0
            ) {
                chunk.dispose();
                this.chunkGraph?.setChunkLoaded(chunk.address, null);
                this.chunks.delete(key);

                this.meshQueue = this.meshQueue.filter(
                    task => task.key !== key
                );

                this.stats.unloaded++;
                this.stats.loaded--;
            }
        }

        this.stats.pendingMeshes = this.meshQueue.length;

        if (!this.worker) {
            this.processFallbackQueue();
        } else {
            this.pumpWorker();
        }
    }

    getMeshes() {
        return Array.from(this.chunks.values(), function getMesh(chunk) {
            return chunk.mesh;
        }).filter(Boolean);
    }

    setVisible(visible) {
        this.visible = Boolean(visible);
        if (!this.chunks || typeof this.chunks.values !== 'function') return;
        for (const chunk of this.chunks.values()) {
            if (!chunk) continue;
            chunk.visible = this.visible;
            if (chunk.mesh) chunk.mesh.isVisible = this.visible;
        }
    }

    getStats() {
        return {
            ...this.stats,
            currentChunks: this.chunks.size,
            chunkSize: this.chunkSize,
            renderRadius: this.renderRadius,
            worldSize: this.terrain.worldSize,
            workerActive: !!this.worker,
            workerReady: this.workerReady,
            reachableRemoteChunks: this.lastReachableRemoteChunks,
            retainedRemoteChunks: this.remoteChunkRetention.size
        };
    }

    dispose() {
        this.enabled = false;

        if (this.worker) {
            this.worker.terminate();
            this.worker = null;
        }

        for (const chunk of this.chunks.values()) {
            chunk.dispose();
            this.chunkGraph?.setChunkLoaded(chunk.address, null);
        }

        this.chunks.clear();
        this.remoteChunkRetention.clear();
        this.meshQueue.length = 0;

        this.material.dispose();

        this.stats.loaded = 0;
        this.stats.pendingMeshes = 0;
    }

failWorker(reason) {
    console.error(
        "[WorldManager] Worker failure:",
        reason
    );

    this.stats.workerErrors++;

    if (this.workerInitTimer) {
        clearTimeout(this.workerInitTimer);
        this.workerInitTimer = null;
    }

    const failedTask = this.currentTask;
    this.currentTask = null;

    if (this.worker) {
        this.worker.terminate();
        this.worker = null;
    }

    this.workerReady = false;
    this.workerBusy = false;

    // Retry the outstanding task synchronously.
    if (failedTask) {
        const chunk = this.chunks.get(failedTask.key);

        if (
            chunk &&
            !chunk.disposed &&
            chunk.revision === failedTask.revision
        ) {
            this.meshQueue.unshift(failedTask);
        }
    }

    this.stats.pendingMeshes = this.meshQueue.length;
    this.processFallbackQueue();
}
}
